        Idea:
Integrate the video editor from announcement-video into reigh-app as a shared component with two display modes (compact EditorPane + full standalone page), Supabase-backed data layer (timelines + effects tables), SupabaseDataProvider, custom effects system with DB storage, and realtime integration

User notes and answers:
- ## Full Implementation Context

### Source Codebase (announcement-video)
- Video editor at: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/viewer/src/tools/video-editor/
- Shared compositions/effects/types at: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/shared/
- React 19 + Remotion 4.0.434 + @xzdarcy/react-timeline-editor 1.0.0 + TanStack React Query
- 3-panel resizable layout: preview (Remotion Player), properties sidebar, timeline
- DataProvider interface: loadTimeline(), saveTimeline(), loadAssetRegistry(), resolveAssetUrl(), uploadAsset()
- Two existing implementations: LocalDataProvider (Vite dev server), ApiDataProvider (stub)
- Timeline config stored as JSON: { output, clips, tracks, customEffects }
- Asset registry: { assets: Record<string, { file, type, duration, resolution, fps }> }
- Effects system: 22 built-in (11 entrance, 6 exit, 5 continuous) + 4 transitions
- Custom effects: source code strings compiled at runtime via sucrase, executed in sandboxed new Function() with injected globals (React, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig, interpolate, spring, AbsoluteFill)
- Dynamic effect registry merges built-in + custom effects
- Draft effects stored in localStorage, promoted to timeline config
- LLM prompt template exists for AI-generated effects
- Serialization validates allowed fields before save

### Target Codebase (reigh-app)
- React 18.3 + Vite + TypeScript + TanStack React Query + Tailwind + shadcn/ui
- Remotion 4.0.434 compatible with React 18 (peer dep >= 16.8.0)
- Supabase backend with JSONB columns pattern (tasks.params, projects.settings, generations.params)
- Existing tables: users, projects, tasks, generations, generation_variants, shots, shot_generations, resources, credits_ledger, workers
- Supabase Storage bucket: image_uploads
- Realtime: RealtimeConnection subscribes to postgres_changes, RealtimeEventProcessor batches events, DataFreshnessManager manages polling
- Tool pattern: src/tools/{toolName}/pages/ + components/ + hooks/
- Tool registration: toolManifest.ts (ToolUIDefinition), toolIds.ts, routes.tsx
- Pane system: PanesContext, useSlidingPane hook, PaneControlTab component
- EditorPane exists but content is EMPTY - slides down from top, 75% viewport height
- EditorPaneControlTab has stubbed TODO for Go to editor button
- Layout: GlobalHeader + Outlet + EditorPane(top) + TasksPane(right) + ToolsPane(left) + GenerationsPane(bottom)

### Data Architecture Decisions
1. timelines table: id, project_id, user_id, name, config (JSONB), asset_registry (JSONB), created_at, updated_at
2. effects table: id, user_id, name, slug, code (text), category (enum), description, is_public, created_at, updated_at
3. SupabaseDataProvider implements DataProvider interface
4. Custom effects referenced by slug in clips (custom:slug), fetched from effects table at load time
5. Built-in effects ship with app code, not in DB
6. Realtime subscriptions on timelines table for collaborative editing

### Two Display Modes
1. EditorPane (compact): Small video preview + basic playback controls, no timeline/properties. Shows in top sliding pane.
2. Standalone page (/tools/video-editor): Full 3-panel editor with preview, timeline, properties panel.
- CRITICAL FORMAT REQUIREMENT: Steps MUST use ## Step N: (h2 level), NOT ### Step N: (h3). Do NOT use ## Phase headers that push steps to h3. Use flat ## Step 1:, ## Step 2:, etc. at h2 level. You can mention phases in step titles like '## Step 1: [Phase 1] Install dependencies' but the heading must be ## not ###.
- MISSING DETAILS TO INCORPORATE INTO THE PLAN:

1. SHARED COMPOSITIONS: The source has shared/compositions/ with TimelineRenderer.tsx, VisualClip.tsx, TextClip.tsx, AudioTrack.tsx, DebugTrack.tsx. These are the core Remotion components that render the video. They must be ported and placed alongside the editor (e.g. src/tools/video-editor/compositions/). Step 3 must explicitly call these out.

2. EFFECT ARCHITECTURE DETAILS:
   - 3 categories: entrances (11: slide-up, slide-down, slide-left, slide-right, zoom-in, zoom-spin, pulse, fade, flip, bounce, meteorite), exits (6: slide-down, zoom-out, flip, fade-out, shrink, dissolve), continuous (5: ken-burns, float, glitch, slow-zoom, drift)
   - 4 transitions (CSS-based, not React components): crossfade, wipe, slide-push, zoom-through
   - wrapWithClipEffects() nesting order: continuous (outer) → entrance → exit (inner) → children
   - custom: prefix convention for clip effect references (e.g. "custom:sparkle")
   - EffectComponentProps interface: { children, durationInFrames, effectFrames?, intensity? }
   - Effects are compiled via sucrase with 6 injected globals: React, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig, interpolate, spring, AbsoluteFill

3. CONFIG UTILS & SERIALIZATION: Must port shared/config-utils.ts (parseResolution, getClipSourceDuration, getClipTimelineDuration, getClipDurationInFrames, getTimelineDurationInFrames, secondsToFrames, resolveTimelineConfig, getConfigSignature) and shared/serialize.ts (serializeForDisk with TIMELINE_CLIP_FIELDS and TRACK_DEFINITION_FIELDS whitelists, validateSerializedConfig).

4. THREE CONTEXT ARCHITECTURE: The source editor uses 3 separate React contexts - TimelineEditorContext (clip selection, timeline edits), TimelineChromeContext (save status, render status), TimelinePlaybackContext (current time, player ref). These should be preserved in the port.

5. EFFECTS TABLE CATEGORY: The category column should be an enum of 'entrance' | 'exit' | 'continuous', NOT 'custom'. Fix the migration: category text NOT NULL CHECK (category IN ('entrance', 'exit', 'continuous'))

6. DEBOUNCE SAVE: The 500ms debounce save pattern is already built into the source's useTimelineData.ts hook. Port it, don't reimplement. It also has external change detection via config signature comparison (getConfigSignature).

7. KEYBOARD SHORTCUTS: Source has useKeyboardShortcuts hook with: Space (play/pause), Arrow keys (navigate), S (split clip at playhead), M (mute/unmute), Delete/Backspace (delete clip), Arrow Up/Down (move clip to adjacent track), Escape (deselect).

8. OVERLAY EDITOR: Source has OverlayEditor.tsx for visual in-canvas editing - bounding boxes for clips, drag to move, corner resize, double-click text clips for inline editing. Must be ported.

9. DATAROVIDER OPTIONAL METHODS: The DataProvider interface also has optional loadWaveform?(assetId) and loadAssetProfile?(assetId) methods. These can be stubbed in SupabaseDataProvider for V1.

10. DYNAMIC EFFECT REGISTRY: The DynamicEffectRegistry class (shared/effects/dynamic-registry.ts) merges built-in + dynamic effects with built-in priority. Methods: register, registerAsync, get, getCode, listAll, isDynamic, getAllDynamicCode, unregister. This is the core runtime that custom effects plug into. Port it.

11. EFFECT STORE: The effect-store.ts has draft effects in localStorage (key: "video-editor:draft-effects") with loadDraftEffects, saveDraftEffect, deleteDraftEffect, promoteDraftToTimeline functions. Port and adapt - promotion should save to effects DB table instead of just timeline config.

12. LLM PROMPT TEMPLATE: effect-prompt-template.ts has getEffectPromptTemplate(description) that generates structured prompts for AI effect generation. Port as static asset.
- REVISION GUIDANCE FOR ALL 6 FLAGS:

1. structure-flat-step-headings: Use FLAT ## Step N: headings. NO ## Phase headers. Group steps logically by naming them e.g. "## Step 1: [Foundation] Install dependencies" but keep ALL steps at h2 level.

2. schema-source-model-mismatch: Use the EXACT source types from announcement-video/shared/types.ts. The TimelineClip uses: id, at, track, clipType (media|hold|text), asset, from, to, speed, hold, volume, x, y, width, height, opacity, text (TextClipData), entrance ({type,duration}), exit ({type,duration}), continuous ({type,intensity?}), transition ({type,duration}), effects. TimelineOutput uses: resolution (string like "1280x720"), fps, file, background?, background_scale?. TrackDefinition uses: id, kind (visual|audio), label, scale?, fit?, opacity?, blendMode?. DO NOT invent new field names.

3. provider-contract-and-query-scope-mismatch: The source DataProvider has NO timelineId params - it's a singleton. For reigh-app we need to EXTEND the interface to support multiple timelines. The plan should explicitly call out: (a) adding timelineId to loadTimeline/saveTimeline/loadAssetRegistry, (b) changing resolveAssetUrl to async (returns Promise<string> for signed URLs), (c) changing uploadAsset return type to { assetId: string, entry: AssetRegistryEntry }, (d) updating query keys from singleton ['timeline-data'] to ['timeline', timelineId].

4. asset-panel-and-timeline-ui-omission: Explicitly list ALL source UI components that need porting: AssetPanel (upload/browse assets), ClipPanel (tabbed: effects/timing/position/audio/text), ClipAction (rendered clip actions in timeline), TrackLabel, cross-track drag via useCrossTrackDrag hook, timeline CSS overrides for @xzdarcy/react-timeline-editor styling.

5. standalone-route-editor-pane-conflict: On /tools/video-editor route, auto-close and hide the EditorPane. Add route awareness to PanesContext or EditorPaneTab - when pathname is /tools/video-editor, suppress the EditorPane control tab and surface entirely. Pattern: check useLocation().pathname in EditorPaneSurface and return null if on the editor page.

6. untrusted-db-effects-execution: For V1, scope custom effects to owner-only (remove is_public or set it to always false). Users can only execute their own effects. Public effect sharing is a V2 feature that requires sandboxing (iframe or Web Worker). Add a note in the plan about this security decision.

    },

      "verdict": ""
    },

      "id": "SC12",
      "task_id": "T12",
      "question": "Does CompactPreview show video with playback controls in the EditorPane? Does 'Open in editor' navigate to /tools/video-editor?timeline={id}? Does the compact pane use the same query cache as full mode (no re-fetch on mode switch)?",
      "verdict": ""
    },
    {
      "id": "SC13",
      "task_id": "T13",
      "question": "Does editing a clip in one browser tab cause the change to appear in another tab within ~2 seconds? When local has unsaved changes and remote changes arrive, does a merge/discard prompt appear (not silent overwrite)?",
      "verdict": ""
    },
    {
      "id": "SC14",
      "task_id": "T14",
      "question": "Does the full E2E flow work: create timeline \u2192 upload asset \u2192 add clip \u2192 apply effect \u2192 save \u2192 reload \u2192 verify persisted correctly with source field names \u2192 open in compact mode \u2192 verify same data?",
      "verdict": ""
    }
  ],
  "meta_commentary": "EXECUTION GUIDANCE:\n\n**Parallelization:** T1, T2, T3 are fully independent \u2014 run in parallel. T4 needs T1 (Remotion types) but non-Remotion types can start immediately. T5, T6, T7 all depend on T4 but are independent of each other \u2014 parallelize. T10 depends on T2+T3+T4 and can run in parallel with T5/T6/T7. T8 is the critical path bottleneck (depends on T5+T6+T7). T9 can parallel with T8.\n\n**CRITICAL: Source types mismatch (W1).** The plan's inline TypeScript snippets in Step 4 have two known inaccuracies vs the actual source: customEffects should be Record<string, CustomEffectEntry> not an array, and background_scale should be number|null not string. The executor MUST read announcement-video/shared/types.ts directly and use those types. This cascades into serialization whitelists, effect loading, and resolveTimelineConfig. If the executor copies the plan snippets verbatim, it will cause runtime bugs in effect registry population and config round-tripping.\n\n**Tool registration (T11) has 6 touch points**, not the 4 in the original plan. The full list: toolIds.ts, toolManifest.ts, tools/index.ts, toolDefaultsRegistry.ts, settingsIds.ts, routes.tsx. Missing any causes runtime settings errors or route validation failures. Read src/docs/structure_detail/adding_new_tool.md and follow an existing tool's pattern (e.g., look at how edit-video is registered across all files).\n\n**EditorPane suppression (T11):** The return-null approach in EditorPaneTab works but verify it suppresses both the pane content AND the control tab (PaneControlTab). If they're rendered separately, both need the pathname check. Look at how Layout.tsx renders EditorPane to understand the component hierarchy.\n\n**resolveAssetUrl is async in reigh-app but sync in source.** Every call site in compositions (VisualClip, AudioTrack) that uses resolveAssetUrl must be adapted. In Remotion compositions, you can't use async directly \u2014 use React Query or state to pre-resolve URLs before render. The source likely passes resolved URLs as props; verify this pattern.\n\n**Order of porting compositions (T5):** Port TimelineRenderer first as it's the root. Then VisualClip (most complex with effects), then TextClip, AudioTrack, DebugTrack. Test each in isolation with a Player before composing.\n\n**Custom effects security boundary:** The RLS policy on effects table is the primary enforcement. App-level queries should use .eq('user_id', userId) as belt-and-suspenders. Never add a policy for is_public=true reads in V1. The is_public column exists for V2 schema forward-compatibility only."
}

        Sense checks to keep in mind during execution (reviewer will verify these):
- SC1 (T1): Does the dev server start and render both Remotion Player and @xzdarcy/react-timeline-editor without console errors? Does npm run build succeed without >200KB gzipped increase?
- SC2 (T2): Do regenerated types.ts include timelines and effects tables with correct column types? Is the effects category CHECK constraint ('entrance','exit','continuous') and is there NO public-read RLS policy on effects?
- SC3 (T3): Can a test upload/download to timeline-assets succeed with the user_id path prefix? Does RLS block access to other users' paths?
- SC4 (T4): Do the ported types match the ACTUAL source types.ts (not plan snippets)? Specifically: is customEffects a Record (not array)? Is background_scale number|null (not string)? Does getConfigSignature produce consistent hashes? Does serializeForDisk strip non-whitelisted fields?
- SC5 (T5): Does TimelineRenderer correctly render clips positioned by clip.at in a Remotion Player? Does wrapWithClipEffects apply effects in continuous→entrance→exit→children order? Are any React 19 APIs properly adapted?
- SC6 (T6): Does DynamicEffectRegistry.get('fade') return built-in even when a custom 'fade' exists? Does get('custom:test') resolve a registered custom effect? Does compileEffect handle errors gracefully (red overlay, not crash)?
- SC7 (T7): Does VideoEditorProvider compose all 3 contexts correctly? Can a test component access TimelineEditorContext, TimelineChromeContext, and TimelinePlaybackContext state via useContext?
- SC8 (T8): Do all source UI components have counterparts: AssetPanel (upload+browse), ClipPanel (5 tabs), ClipAction (timeline renderer), TrackLabel, OverlayEditor (drag/resize/inline-edit), useCrossTrackDrag? Does VideoEditorShell render correctly in both full and compact modes?
- SC9 (T9): Are query keys ['timeline', timelineId] (not ['timeline-data'])? Does the debounce save fire once after 500ms of inactivity, not per-change? Do keyboard shortcuts work in editor but not fire when typing in inputs?
- SC10 (T10): Does SupabaseDataProvider round-trip a TimelineConfig through save→load with correct field names in JSONB? Does resolveAssetUrl return a working signed URL? Does uploadAsset return {assetId, entry} and update asset_registry?
- SC11 (T11): Is the tool registered in ALL 6 locations: toolIds.ts, toolManifest.ts, tools/index.ts, toolDefaultsRegistry.ts, settingsIds.ts, routes.tsx? Does EditorPane (and its control tab) disappear on /tools/video-editor and reappear on other routes?
- SC12 (T12): Does CompactPreview show video with playback controls in the EditorPane? Does 'Open in editor' navigate to /tools/video-editor?timeline={id}? Does the compact pane use the same query cache as full mode (no re-fetch on mode switch)?
- SC13 (T13): Does editing a clip in one browser tab cause the change to appear in another tab within ~2 seconds? When local has unsaved changes and remote changes arrive, does a merge/discard prompt appear (not silent overwrite)?
- SC14 (T14): Does the full E2E flow work: create timeline → upload asset → add clip → apply effect → save → reload → verify persisted correctly with source field names → open in compact mode → verify same data?
Watch items to keep visible during execution:
- W1: SCHEMA MISMATCH IN PLAN SNIPPETS — The plan's Step 4 inline code shows customEffects as CustomEffectDefinition[] but source uses Record<string, CustomEffectEntry>. Also shows background_scale as string but source uses number|null. MUST read the actual source file announcement-video/shared/types.ts and use those types, not the plan snippets. Affects serialization whitelists, effect registry loading, and config round-tripping.
- W2: TOOL REGISTRATION COMPLETENESS — Plan Step 11 originally missed toolDefaultsRegistry (src/tooling/toolDefaultsRegistry.ts) and TOOL_SETTINGS_IDS (src/shared/lib/settingsIds.ts). T11 now includes these. Executor must also check if toolRoutes.ts auto-derives from toolRuntimeManifest or needs manual update.
- W3: REACT 18 COMPATIBILITY — Source is React 19. Watch for use() hook (replace with useContext/useSuspenseQuery), React.cache (replace with memoization), and any async component patterns. Remotion 4.0.434 itself is compatible (peer dep >=16.8).
- W4: CUSTOM EFFECTS SECURITY — V1 is owner-only. Never query effects with is_public=true. Never compile another user's effect code. RLS enforces at DB level, app must also enforce. Public sharing is V2 requiring iframe/Web Worker sandboxing.
- W5: DATAPROVIDER SINGLETON→MULTI-TIMELINE REFACTOR — Source DataProvider has no timelineId params and uses singleton query keys. Every hook/component that touches DataProvider must be adapted: add timelineId params, update query keys from ['timeline-data'] to ['timeline', timelineId], make resolveAssetUrl async.
- W6: EXISTING edit-video TOOL — New video-editor coexists with existing edit-video tool at /tools/edit-video (TOOL_IDS.EDIT_VIDEO). Different purposes. Don't accidentally overwrite or conflict.
- W7: EDITOR PANE SUPPRESSION — Layout.tsx always renders <EditorPane/>. Must suppress on /tools/video-editor route via pathname check in EditorPaneTab.tsx returning null. Test that control tab also disappears, not just content.
- W8: DEBOUNCE SAVE — Port the existing 500ms debounce from source useTimelineData.ts. Don't reimplement. It uses getConfigSignature for change detection. External change detection (remote vs local signature comparison) is needed for realtime merge UX.
- W9: EFFECT NESTING ORDER — wrapWithClipEffects must be continuous(outer)→entrance→exit(inner)→children. Reversing this breaks all effect animations. Verify with a clip that has all three effect types.
- W10: REALTIME CHANNEL LIMITS — Adding timelines to the existing channel's postgres_changes subscriptions. Supabase has a limit of ~10 filters per channel. Current count is 4 tables. Adding 3 more events (INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE for timelines) brings it to ~10. Monitor for channel subscription failures.

Cross-tab behavior was implemented in code but not interactively verified in a browser session here.","files_changed":["src/shared/hooks/useRealtimeInvalidation.ts","src/shared/realtime/RealtimeConnection.ts","src/shared/realtime/RealtimeEventProcessor.ts","src/shared/realtime/index.ts","src/shared/realtime/types.ts","src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineRealtime.ts"],"commands_run":["npm run build"]},{"task_id":"T14","status":"skipped","executor_notes":"Full browser/Supabase E2E validation was blocked by the sandbox: local port binding for the dev server is denied, and live Supabase push/upload/realtime sessions were unavailable. I validated with repeated production builds plus targeted Vitest coverage for serialization, effect registry behavior, provider composition, and tool registration.","files_changed":[],"commands_run":[]}],"sense_check_acknowledgments":[{"sense_check_id":"SC1","executor_note":"`npm run build` passed and the new tool bundles cleanly; the current main gzip artifact was ~910 KB versus the earlier pre-integration build at ~758 KB, so the inferred increase stayed below 200 KB. Interactive dev-server/browser rendering was not verified because local port binding is denied in this sandbox (`npm run dev` failed with `listen EPERM`)."},{"sense_check_id":"SC2","executor_note":"The SQL migration adds `timelines` and `effects`, uses the exact `category IN ('entrance','exit','continuous')` check, and does not add any public-read policy for `effects`. `src/integrations/supabase/types.ts` was updated manually because live type generation could not run here."},{"sense_check_id":"SC3","executor_note":"The `timeline-assets` bucket migration and per-user folder policies were authored, but I could not run live upload/download or cross-user policy checks without a reachable Supabase environment."},{"sense_check_id":"SC4","executor_note":"`src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts` uses the source-ground-truth shape (`customEffects` as a record and numeric `background_scale`), and `src/tools/video-editor/lib/serialize.test.ts` verifies whitelist stripping and valid serialization."},{"sense_check_id":"SC5","executor_note":"The compositions were ported and the effects wrapper order in `src/tools/video-editor/effects/index.tsx` remains continuous → entrance → exit → children. This was validated by code inspection plus successful build, not by a browser-rendered Remotion preview session."},{"sense_check_id":"SC6","executor_note":"`src/tools/video-editor/effects/DynamicEffectRegistry.test.tsx` covers built-in priority over dynamic collisions, `custom:` lookup normalization, and non-throwing invalid custom-effect registration. The red overlay render path itself was not exercised in jsdom because Remotion resolves to a second React copy there."},{"sense_check_id":"SC7","executor_note":"`src/tools/video-editor/contexts/VideoEditorProvider.test.tsx` verifies that `VideoEditorProvider` composes editor, chrome, and playback contexts and exposes all three to a child component."},{"sense_check_id":"SC8","executor_note":"Counterpart files now exist for AssetPanel, ClipPanel, ClipAction, TrackLabel, OverlayEditor, `useCrossTrackDrag`, and the shared shell with compact/full modes. This was implementation-checked and build-validated rather than visually QA'd in a browser."},{"sense_check_id":"SC9","executor_note":"The hook layer uses `['timeline', timelineId]` and `['asset-registry', timelineId]`, and `useTimelineData` preserves the 500ms debounced save/signature flow. Keyboard shortcuts were updated in code for play/pause, left/right seek, split, mute, delete, up/down track move, and escape, but not interactively browser-tested here."},{"sense_check_id":"SC10","executor_note":"`SupabaseDataProvider` serializes exact source field names, returns signed URLs, and merges uploaded asset entries into `asset_registry`. Live save/load/upload verification against Supabase was not possible in the sandbox."},{"sense_check_id":"SC11","executor_note":"`src/tools/video-editor/registration.test.ts` covers tool ID, runtime manifest, tool routes, tools manifest, defaults registry, and settings IDs, and `src/app/routes.tsx` now mounts `/tools/video-editor`. `EditorPaneTab.tsx` suppresses both the tab and surface on the standalone route; that suppression was code-reviewed and build-validated, not browser-clicked."},{"sense_check_id":"SC12","executor_note":"Compact mode is wired through the shared provider/query keys and opens `/tools/video-editor?timeline={id}` from the pane. Shared-cache behavior was implemented via identical query keys, but mode-switch behavior was not browser-profiled in this sandbox."},{"sense_check_id":"SC13","executor_note":"Realtime subscriptions, processed `timelines-updated` events, cache invalidation, and a keep-local/discard dialog were implemented. Cross-tab propagation timing and conflict prompts were not exercised in a live browser session."},{"sense_check_id":"SC14","executor_note":"The full browser/Supabase E2E flow was not executed here. Validation was limited to successful production builds and targeted unit tests, so this end-to-end sense check remains unverified in a live environment."}]}
